# Historical Technical Library: Engineering, Technology and the Applied Sciences Before 1900

> A catalog of documents intended to reconstruct the **technical knowledge, engineering methods, production processes, machinery, and technological capabilities** available before  1900 D.C.

## Ars Antiqua

> The classical and Hellenistic technical canon - the machine books, water trades and stone craft that stand at the beginning of every later engineering science, carried here as the pre-1500 foundations besid the later Renaissance descendants of the same families.

| Author | Work | Year | Technical domain |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Ahmes (Rhind Mathematical Papyrus); Moscow Mathematical Papyrus | *Problems of granaries, frustums, pyramid slopes, seked and mensuration* | c. 1650–1550 BCE | Problem-book of the Egyptian technical arithmetic and mensuration |
| Nineveh cuneiform glass and metal tablets (Library of Ashurbanipal) | *Furnace recipes and coloured glazes* | c. 650 BCE | The glass and glaze trade preserved in the ancient libraries |
| Pseudo-Aristotle (Strato / Peripatetic school) | *Mechanica (Mechanical Problems)* | c. 300 BCE | The lever, pulley, wheel, wedge, roller, rudder and oar stated by question |
| Archimedes | [*On the Equilibrium of Planes; On Floating Bodies; Measurement of a Circle*](/view.html?p=documents/books/archimedes-1897-works-heath.md) | c. 250 BCE (Eng. ed. 1897) | Statics and hydrostatics with the stability of ship hulls — the Heath edition of the treatises |
| Ctesibius of Alexandria (via Vitruvius/Philo/Hero) | *Force pump, water organ, and feedback-regulated clepsydra* | fl. 270 BCE | Pneumatic and water-regulated machinery of the Alexandrian shops |
| Philo of Byzantium | *Mechanike Syntaxis (Belopoeica; Pneumatica; Poliorcetica)* | c. 230 BCE | The artillery, siphons, and level-controls of the Hellenistic defensive craft |
| Biton | *Kataskeuai polemikon organon kai katapeltikon* | c. 240 BCE | The scaled siege-machines written with dimensions |
| Hero (Heron) | [*Pneumatica; Mechanica; Automata; Dioptra; Belopoeica; Cheiroballistra; Metrica*](/view.html?p=documents/books/hero-1899-druckwerke.md) | fl. 60 CE (ed. 1899) | The chef d'oeuvre of Hellenistic mechanics — the aeolipile reaction steam sphere, the five machine laws, the Mechanica preserved in Qusta ibn Luqa's Arabic, automata and surveying dioptra (Schmidt's 1899 edition of the Druckwerke) |
| Claudius Ptolemy | *Almagest; Geographia; Optica; Planisphaerium* | fl. 150 CE | The astronomical and cartographic instruments of the conic projections |
| Pappus of Alexandria | *Synagoge (Book VIII)* | fl. 320 CE | The synthesis of ancient mechanics with the inclined plane and worm gear |
| Cato; Varro; Columella; Palladius | *De Agri Cultura; Res Rusticae; De Re Rustica; Opus Agriculturae* | 160 BCE – 400 CE | The farm mechanics, presses and drainage of the Roman estate economy |
| Sextus Julius Frontinus | *De Aquaeductu Urbae Romae* | c. 97 CE | The Roman water supply gauged at its aqueduct intakes |
| Pedanius Dioscorides | *De Materia Medica* | c. 65 CE | The medicinal substances and the first apparatus of botanical extraction |
| Pliny | [*Naturalis Historia (Books XXXIII–XXXVII)*](/view.html?p=documents/books/pliny-1855-natural-history.md) | 77 CE (Eng. 1855) | The industrial arts of metals, glass, pigments and papyrus in one encyclopedia — Bostock and Riley's translation |
| Corpus Agrimensorum Romanorum (Hyginus Gromaticus, Balbus, Siculus Flaccus) | *Gromatic surveying by groma and chorobates* | 1st–2nd c. CE | The survey graphic machinery of centuriation and road planning |
| Leyden Papyrus X; Stockholm Papyrus; Zosimos of Panopolis | *Alchemical apparatus recipes and lab practices* | c. 300 CE | The chemical tables, alloys, gilding and distillation of the Alexandrian trades |
| Vegetius; Pseudo-Anonymous | *Epitoma Rei Militaris; De Rebus Bellicis* | c. 390 CE | The Roman fortifications and paddle-wheel warships of the late empire |
| Anthemius of Tralles; Procopius | *On Burning Mirrors; Hagia Sophia dome geometry; De Aedificiis* | 532–550 CE | The great vaulting and dome-engineering of the imperial city |
| Marcus Vitruvius Pollio | *De Architectura Libri Decem* | c. 25 BCE | The classical canon: aqueducts, cranes, watermills and theatre acoustics (see the 1826 Gwilt translation in Architectura) |
| Aeneas Tacticus; Athenaeus Mechanicus; Apollodorus of Damascus; Onasander | *Poliorcetica (c. 350 BCE — hydraulic water-clock telegraphy, ciphers and mine counter-tunnelling); Peri Mechanematon (1st c. BCE siege tortoises and rams); Poliorcetica (c. 105 CE — Trajan's Danube bridge and siege towers); Strategikos* | 350 BCE - 115 CE | Greek and Roman siege engineering, field bridging and optical/hydraulic signalling (no scan verified) |
| Sextus Julius Africanus; Oribasius of Pergamon | *Kestoi (c. 220 CE — military pyrotechnics, surveying and metrology); Collectiones Medicae Book XLIX (4th c. CE — Alexandrian surgical traction machines, bone-setting windlasses and the Glossokomon compound pulley reduction frame)* | c. 220-380 CE | Late antique pyrotechnic recipes and mechanical orthopaedic reduction engines (no scan verified) |

## Ars Mediaevalia

> The medieval and Renaissance European technical literature - the manuscript trades and printed canon between Theophilus and the printed machine books of the sixteenth century.

| Author | Work | Year | Technical domain |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Mappae Clavicula; Heraclius | *De Coloribus et Artibus Romanorum; the craft recipe manuscripts* | c. 800–1130 | The pigment, alloy and coating recipes of monastic workshops |
| Theophilus Presbyter | *Schedula Diversarum Artium (De Diversis Artibus)* | c. 1122 | The metalwork, stained glass, organ pipe and bell founding trades described (no scan verified) |
| Villard de Honnecourt | *Livre de Portraiture (Sketchbook)* | c. 1220–40 | The Gothic trades and machinery carried in one sketchbook of engineering |
| Petrus Peregrinus de Maricourt | *Epistola de Magnete* | 1269 | The compass and magnet designed by experiment |
| Guido da Vigevano | *Texaurus Regis Francie* | 1335 | Cranked machinery of the siege hospital trades |
| Giovanni Dondi dell Orologio | *Tractatus Astrarii* | 1364–89 | The planetary clock with its escapement and gear-plates figured |
| Geoffrey Chaucer | *A Treatise on the Astrolabe* | 1391 | The first technical instrument science book to be written in English |
| Konrad Kyeser; Anonymous | *Bellifortis; Feuerwerkbuch von 1420* | 1405 / 1420 | The mechanical siege arts of gunpowder at the cannons and the powdermakers |
| Giovanni Fontana | *Bellicorum Instrumentorum Liber* | c. 1420 | The marvel machines of rockets and mechanical lights designed for the courts |
| Mariano di Jacopo detto il Taccola | *De Ingeneis; De Machinis* | 1419–49 | The pumps, cranes and harvest machinery drawn by the Sienese engineer |
| Filarete (Antonio Averlino) | *Trattato di Architettura* | c. 1461–64 | The architectural and engineering theory of the iron blast furnace and finery forge |
| Roberto Valturio | *De Re Militari (Verona)* | 1472 | The first printed book with technical woodcuts and siege engines |
| Francesco di Giorgio Martini (Promis ed. 1841) | *Trattati di Architettura, Ingegneria e Arte Militare* | c. 1475–90 | The bastion trace and machinery of the Renaissance military engineer |
| Luca Pacioli | *Summa de Arithmetica (Particularis de Computis et Scripturis)* | 1494 | Double-entry bookkeeping and the accounting of industrial enterprise |
| Leonardo da Vinci | [*Codice Atlantico and the Manuscripts*](/view.html?p=documents/books/leonardo-1883-literary-works.md) | c. 1478–1519 (ed. 1883) | The machine elements and the flying machines of the great engineer — Richter's edition of the notebooks |
| Ulrich Rulein von Calw | *Ein nützlich Bergbüchlein (c. 1500); Probierbüchlein (c. 1520)* | c. 1500 / c. 1520 | The first printed mining and assaying manuals |
| Adelard of Bath; Jordanus de Nemore; Roger Bacon | *Quaestiones Naturales & Euclid/Khwarizmi translations (12th c.); Liber de Ratione Ponderis (13th c. — positional gravity, bent lever and virtual displacements on the inclined plane); Epistola de Secretis Operibus Artis et Naturae (c. 1260 — eyeglasses, gunpowder and mechanical propulsion)* | 1120-1270 | Medieval statics of the inclined plane by virtual work, optical lenses and early European gunpowder (no scan verified) |
| Richard of Wallingford | *Tractatus Horologii Astronomici (1327-30)* | 1327-30 | The St Albans astronomical clock with double-pallet strob escapement, fusee-like spiral barrel and differential gear trains (no scan verified) |
| Cennino d Andrea Cennini; Lorenzo Ghiberti; Piero della Francesca | *Il Libro dell Arte (c. 1400 — gesso, tempera, mordant gilding, fresco and life-casting); Commentarii (c. 1447 — lost-wax monumental bronze casting); De Prospectiva Pingendi & Libellus de Quinque Corporibus Regularibus (c. 1474-82)* | 1400-82 | Workshop treatise of the Italian painter-gilder, lost-wax bronze founding and projective geometry (no scan verified) |

## Ars Mechanica

> From the individual mechanical invention to the systematic representation of machines, mechanisms, production operations and machine design. The *Encyclopédie* is the hinge: its technical plates document workshops, tools, production equipment and manufacturing operations.

| Author | Work | Year | Technical domain |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Fausto Veranzio | [*Machinae Novae*](/view.html?p=documents/books/veranzio-1615-machinae-novae.md) | 1615–16 | Mechanical inventions |
| Georg A. Böckler | [*Theatrum Machinarum Novum*](/view.html?p=documents/books/bockler-1661-theatrum-machinarum-novum.md) | 1661 | Machinery and mechanisms |
| Bernard Forest de Bélidor | [*Architecture Hydraulique*](/view.html?p=documents/books/belidor-1737-architecture-hydraulique.md) | 1737–53 | Hydraulic machinery |
| B. F. de Bélidor | [*La Science des Ingénieurs*](/view.html?p=documents/books/belidor-1729-science-des-ingenieurs.md) | 1729 | The engineer's arithmetic codified before the corps schools |
| Denis Diderot & Jean le Rond d'Alembert | [*Encyclopédie: Recueil de planches*](/view.html?p=documents/books/diderot-1772-encyclopedie-planches.md) | 1762–72 | Mechanical arts and manufacturing |
| Charles Babbage | [*On the Economy of Machinery and Manufactures*](/view.html?p=documents/books/babbage-1832-economy-of-machinery-and-manufactures.md) | 1832 | Manufacturing systems and production organization |
| William J. M. Rankine | [*A Manual of Machinery and Millwork*](/view.html?p=documents/books/rankine-1869-manual-of-machinery-and-millwork.md) | 1869 | Machine construction |
| Franz Reuleaux | [*The Kinematics of Machinery*](/view.html?p=documents/books/reuleaux-1875-kinematics-of-machinery.md) | 1875 | Mechanism theory and machine design |
| J. T. Desaguliers | [*A Course of Experimental Philosophy*](/view.html?p=documents/books/desaguliers-1734-course-of-experimental-philosophy.md) | 1734–45 | Experimental philosophy taught as machine theory and practical machine use |
| Jacob Leupold | [*Theatrum Machinarum Generale*](/view.html?p=documents/books/leupold-1724-theatrum-machinarum-generale.md) | 1724 | General machine science |
| Jacob Leupold | [*Theatrum Machinarum Hydrotechnicarum*](/view.html?p=documents/books/leupold-1724-theatrum-machinarum-hydrotechnicarum.md) | 1724–25 | Hydraulic machinery, waterworks and lifting systems |
| Jacob Leupold | [*Theatrum Machinarum Molarium*](/view.html?p=documents/books/leupold-1735-theatrum-machinarum-molarium.md) | 1735 | Mills and mechanical power transmission |
| Louis Hachette | [*Traité Élémentaire des Machines*](/view.html?p=documents/books/hachette-1819-traite-elementaire-des-machines.md) | 1819 | Machine components and elementary machine theory |
| Claude-Louis Navier | [*Résumé des Leçons données à l'École des Ponts et Chaussées*](/view.html?p=documents/books/navier-1826-resume-des-lecons.md) | 1826 | Applied mechanics and engineering calculation |
| Peter Nicholson | [*The Mechanic's Companion*](/view.html?p=documents/books/nicholson-1831-mechanics-companion.md) | 1831 | Practical mechanics, tools and mechanical operations |
| Charles Holtzapffel | [*Turning and Mechanical Manipulation* (5 vols.)](/view.html?p=documents/books/holtzapffel-1843-turning-and-mechanical-manipulation.md) | 1843–84 | Lathes, cutting tools, turning and workshop practice |
| Charles Holtzapffel & Oliver Byrne | [*The Handbook for the Artisan, Mechanic, and Engineer*](/view.html?p=documents/books/holtzapffel-byrne-1853-handbook-artisan.md) | 1853 | Practical mechanical construction and workshop techniques |
| Thomas Box | [*A Practical Treatise on Mill-Gearing, Wheels, Shafts, Riggers*](/view.html?p=documents/books/box-1853-mill-gearing.md) | 1853 | Mechanical transmission and gearing |
| J. E. Armengaud (tr. William Johnson) | [*The Practical Draughtsman's Book of Industrial Design*](/view.html?p=documents/books/armengaud-1865-practical-draughtsmans-book.md) | 1865 | Mechanical drawing and industrial design |
| Joseph Whitworth | [*Miscellaneous Papers on Mechanical Subjects*](/view.html?p=documents/books/whitworth-1858-miscellaneous-papers.md) | 1858 | Precision engineering: the plane surface and the screw-thread standard |
| C. P. B. Shelley | [*Workshop Appliances*](/view.html?p=documents/books/shelley-1897-workshop-appliances.md) | 1897 | Gauging, measuring, cutting tools, lathes and the machine-tool register |
| E. A. Rix & A. Chodzko | [*A Practical Treatise on Compressed Air and Pneumatic Machinery*](/view.html?p=documents/books/rix-1896-compressed-air-pneumatic-machinery.md) | 1896 | Air compression, blowers and pneumatic power transmission |

| Salomon de Caus | [*Les Raisons des Forces Mouvantes*](/view.html?p=documents/books/decaus-1615-raisons-des-forces-mouvantes.md) | 1615 | The physics of motion applied — the machine book at its theoretical hinge |

> From practical cultivation techniques toward experimentally grounded agricultural science: Davy for the chemical composition of soils and plants, Liebig for plant nutrition, Johnston for the geology of soils, Johnson for growth and feeding.

## Ars Civilis

> Structural materials, construction and the engineering profession's manuals. Tredgold's cast-iron tract shows engineering moving from empirical construction practice toward experimentally established material properties and quantitative structural design. Military engineering, once a branch of the same profession, is gathered in its own section.

## Ars Structura

> The engineering science layer beneath Ars Civilis — the strength of materials, the statics of frames, channel and pipe hydraulics and the new cements and concrete, computed from Galileo's beam to the steel skeleton and standing above the Tredgold–Rankine teaching out of which the profession manuals are written. Civil-engineering science rather than the Ars Civilis profession itself.

| Author | Work | Year | Technical domain |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Isaac Newton | [*Philosophiae Naturalis Principia Mathematica*](/view.html?p=documents/books/newton-1687-principia-mathematica.md) | 1687 (ed. 1822) | The laws of motion and the fluid dynamics of Book II — the founding mechanics |
| Sextus Julius Frontinus (tr. Clemens Herschel) | [*The Two Books on the Water Supply of the City of Rome*](/view.html?p=documents/books/frontinus-1899-water-supply-rome.md) | c. 97 (tr. 1899) | The Roman water service measured at its intakes — the classical waterworks administration |
| Benedetto Castelli | *Della Misura dell'Acque Correnti* | 1628 | Stream continuity laid down — velocity and section (no scan verified) |
| Galileo Galilei | [*Discorsi e Dimostrazioni Matematiche intorno a due Nuove Scienze*](/view.html?p=documents/books/galileo-1638-discorsi-two-new-sciences.md) | 1638 | The cantilever beam made a problem of strengths — the founding of the strength of materials |
| Domenico Guglielmini | [*Della Natura de' Fiumi*](/view.html?p=documents/books/guglielmini-1697-natura-de-fiumi.md) | 1697 | The river as a computed regime — slope, velocity and bed |
| Daniel Bernoulli | [*Hydrodynamica*](/view.html?p=documents/books/bernoulli-1738-hydrodynamica.md) | 1738 | Pressure, motion and efflux bound in one law — analytical hydrodynamics founded |
| Charles-Augustin de Coulomb | [*Théorie des machines simples (with the 1773 Essai on statics)*](/view.html?p=documents/books/coulomb-1821-theorie-machines-simples.md) | 1773 (ed. 1821) | Earth pressure and masonry friction — the founding statics of soil and arch |

## Ars Electrica

> The experimental foundations of electricity distinguished from the engineering literature the systems built on them: Priestley and Faraday carry the science, Volta the pile, and the telegraphy and electric-light books the systems.

## Ars Instrumentalis

> Instrumentation, measurement and optical technology — distinct technological branches, not subdivisions of general physics: optical instruments, precision mechanical instruments, electrical measuring instruments, surveying and geodetic instruments, thermometry and pressure measurement, acoustic instruments, seismological and geophysical instrumentation.

| Author | Work | Year | Technical domain |
|---|---|---:|---|

| Ibn al-Haytham (Alhazen) | [*Kitāb al-Manāẓir (Book of Optics); Opticae Thesaurus (Risner ed.)*](/view.html?p=documents/books/haytham-1572-opticae-thesaurus.md) | c. 1027 (ed. 1572) | Experimental optics and the camera obscura — the founding book of the European optical trade |
| David Brewster | [*A Treatise on Optics*](/view.html?p=documents/books/brewster-1831-treatise-on-optics.md) | 1831 | Optical instrumentation |
